Astral Visions

Let me explain in a way your brain can ascertain,

The story of how I came down from outer space now.

As I vape this isolated tryptamine to break the tessellated,

Mentally created computer screen—it’s overcomplicated.

But see, the dreams are too much to count,

A great amount if you knew what they’re about.

Cookin’ DMT so easily with some simple chemistry,

We freebased it, escaped it, and faced infinity.

Stimulated energy, blending synergistically,

Coalescing slowly, unlocking infinity’s mystery.

Delivered me directly into the heart of imagery,

Melting into the universal symphony.

We replayed it, arrangement evaluated,

The placement of amazement—a maze that’s man-created.

Yes, I made this in my spaceship, an honest statement,

About the crazed amazement that’s displayed to escape pain quick.

Redirect your course to discover the source,

Of the endless wars—we’re lovers and fighters at our core.

Blessed with introspection, I force this connection,

To guide you forth into the fourth dimension.

My repertoire is bizarre, but it’s hardly hard to catch,

With a large reservoir of attention, here’s the match:

Add through subtraction, subtract through addiction,

Quiet ambition, loud with a wise vision.

Try to listen as I drip rhythm through the treble,

Settle like a pebble underneath the water level.

Exponential graffiti with existential strokes,

Painting truths in the shadows, waking sleeping folks.

Deep in the shallows, believe me, you’ll find me,

Hiding in alcoves, ceremonial tobacco binds me.

This astral projection isn’t a fleeting reflection,

It’s a map to the cosmos, a multidimensional connection.

  
**Verse 1  
**You try to tell me, "Eat healthy, your body’s a temple."

Give me a second, let me fix this table, guys.

A new surprise—I think my booth’s compromised.

By what I assume is therapeutic.

**Verse 2

Hidden in the disillusion, in the quiet peace.

Centuries passed—millennia unfolded,  
The search never ends, horizons remolded.  
To clock the infinite, we must ascend,  
Beyond this existence, where truths extend.

**Verse 1  
**Entertainment fed to us daily,  
A designed distraction, subtle and shady.  
We’re made to feel in touch, yet complacent,  
Good triumphs in stories, but evil stays adjacent.

Our reality—perpetuated by design,  
Crafted by powers hidden behind the line.  
A benevolent front, but behind the curtain,  
A calculated plan with motives uncertain.

Distractions piled high, vague yet effective,  
Subtracting focus, keeping us disconnected.  
Smoke and mirrors, no masks, we choke,  
Slipping into survival mode, losing the scope.

**Hook  
**It’s laughable, isn’t it? This capitalist game,  
Where progress is stunted, and the rich stake their claim.  
We’ve got the tech to play gods in the sky,  
But we’re chasing illusions, not asking why.

**Verse 2  
**Inconsistencies vivid, waiting to be found,  
While most walk past, eyes glued to the ground.  
Worried about health, insurance, or rent,  
Chained by the system, every dollar spent.

"Buy this, own that, then you'll feel whole,"  
A narrative designed to control your soul.  
Rich and famous—a mirage we pursue,  
A golden carrot dangled, but never true.

Taught that waste is progress, greed is great,  
Conditioned to accept a manipulated fate.  
A game of control, designed to obscure,  
The truth hidden, the motives impure.

**Bridge  
**Blind trust is poison, a trap we defend,  
If it doesn’t add up, they’re lying again.  
We’re left with questions, no answers to find,  
Controlled by illusions, enslaved in the mind.

But we have the tools to break their scheme,  
To unearth the lies and rebuild the dream.  
Expose their motives, reject their control,  
The truth is the key to reclaiming our soul.

**Outro  
**So stop and question, open your eyes,  
Don’t let their distractions cloud the skies.  
Their power relies on division and fear,  
But united, we’re stronger—let’s make that clear.

I found things in front of me, simply astounding,  
Claiming I bring invincibility—now that’s silly, see,  
Improbable possibilities unfold, bending realities,  
Audiences spellbound by a princess vivid with viciousness,  
Hidden under every cheek you dare to check.  
Don’t let me creep past, move through me, equal to Toonsie.

Is it truly that awful to be unremarkable?  
I breathe in the air—oh, au contraire!  
No care in the world, yet the despair it holds,  
Rests like marigolds buried slow, entering through  
Into a hex anew, a shrewd view I leave with you.

Bamboo rises beyond the zoo,  
Apocalyptic truths bloom into incredible hues.  
Indelible, unintelligible waves move through me,  
Frequencies opening epiphanies—  
Opportunities we all can use  
To navigate the ins and outs of differences,  
Finding meaning in the complex similarities  
That weave their way through our realities.

Breathe, plant seeds of anarchy,  
Let them grow into what it behooves you to believe true.  
Underneath, the source springs wonder—  
Blues that blend into infinite hues.  
So unremarkable, yet I’ve got so much heart to grow.  
Options slow, kept close,  
Entrance open—hoping my smoking,  
Choking ways may somehow pave an actual path

**Verse 1  
**Jesus, listen closely to these epiphanies,  
Tucked away, hidden beneath, far from any ease.  
The answers only emerge if you hold the right question,  
A secret code to unlock suppressed dimensions.

Truth is evasive, multi-faceted, mathematical,  
Paradoxical, illogical yet actual.  
If you truly wish to grow, the lies must dissolve,  
But they're deeply rooted, cleverly evolved.

**Hook  
**The system’s designed to keep you blind,  
A maze built to control your mind.  
Fear and trust were always intertwined,  
From the 1600s to these modern times.

**Verse 2  
**Power seeks itself, repeating the same old song,  
Edison robbed Tesla—where did it all go wrong?  
The barons thrive, beating down the honest few,  
Contradictions rule, but the truth breaks through.

Children lost in overcrowded prisons,  
Another cog in the wheel, no one listens.  
The laws they write to cage the masses,  
Feed the rich, as the cycle passes.

Time? It’s nothing but a metaphor,  
A way to score a game that’s hollow to the core.  
Cosmic insignificance, yet the hatred grows,  
A system designed to divide and impose.

**Bridge  
**Division is their weapon, distraction their tool,  
Bleeding Earth dry while we act like fools.  
Riches hoarded high, but what’s the cost,  
When the air, the sea, and the soil are lost?

They’re not clever, just relentless,  
Fueling hatred to leave us defenseless.  
But I dream of a team, a clean-up mission,  
To dismantle the lies, expose the vision.

**Verse 3  
**Taxes claimed for protection, yet it’s just a disguise,  
Guarding wealth from the people they despise.  
Violence hidden, but souls compromised,  
A program of control, deeply institutionalized.

Technology’s here, but let’s get it straight—  
It’s not just a tool, it’s the key to break the hate.  
Yet actors planted, spreading fear and lies,  
Distract us from truth, keep us blind to the skies.

**Outro  
**The stars wrote the story long before we began,  
An eternal script now rests in our hands.  
Let’s rewrite the future, unmask the charade,  
And build a world where love won't fade.

Grewed up a screw-up until I blew up from

Fungus among us—dimethyltryptamine runs.

My vision sees it vividly, but the culture seems blind,

Never interested, never invested in these stories of mine.

Whatever—seems too boring. But here’s a warning:

I’m boiling antifreeze, believe me, it’s alarming.

Antibodies probably responsible for my survival,

These chemicals not killing me yet, my revival’s primal.

I burn deep, a third-degree kind of heat,

Merge with peeps who earn, week after week.

Honest wage-workers learn to creep and burn,

Weekends smoking sherm, seeking lessons unlearned.

Repenting—never felt the reason or season,

Off Robitussin, discussing life’s meaning while I'm leaning.

Spent it at last, surpassed the stress test,

Now they wet you with weapons, keeping you suppressed.

I grow too, rooted like a vegetable—it's beautiful.

Never above you—who am I to judge, dude?

I’ve never seen the sun through another’s eyes,

Never my place to decide their fate or their lies.

Sliding papes by your side, trying not to compromise,

This here’s my disguise—prepare to be hypnotized.

Monetized dreams force plans that ain't mine,

It’s hard to find my heart aligned with this art design.

But when the energy amplifies, the mastermind shines,

Blasting spines after nine on Channel 5 lines.

Baffled as you when taboo avenues

Crowd the room—smooth moves, proud and true.

Found the womb—I mean the woman who runs underground,

For fun, I run, puffing smoke, lost then found.

Choking slow, near overdose levels,

Treble and bass embrace sacred geometry revels.

Make it beyond belief, feet planted firm,

Too honest for the lies—anarchy confirmed.

It surrounds me in the air I breathe,

Dystrophy, atrophy, all the chaos underneath.

No, I’m not mad—just believe what I see:

Demons spun from shadows, conundrums set me free.

What if I’m one? A dry run, crying love,

Laughing loud at the laundromat, dreaming above.

I’m honest after the laughter and the disaster,

Sounds fantastic, but it’s tragic and magic wrapped faster.

Loud spells, dad’s shadow shrouds the static,

**Bridge  
**Enough of this paradise  
That’s only as real as a roll of the dice.

Nearly deceased like a lost tourist in the forest of grief,

In the darkness, but regardless, I sharpen belief.

I take the abuse, let it fuse with the truth that I use,

Refusing to lose as I push through this muse.

The juice is ruthless, flows like rivers of flame,

Aimed to decay the barriers placed in your lane.

This communiqué is made to ricochet thoughts,

Slice through the system like a lyrical Molotov.

Underpaid but I stay with a play and a listen,

Today's mission—rewire your cognition.

Central nervous ignition, my verses—precision,

Each line disperses ancient wisdom.

Fuck imperialism, I'm hacking the prison,

Turn off the television, split the schism, make a decision.

Listen, I’m here for the juxtaposition,

Bringing nuclear fission with rhythm's incision.

Spitting over this instrumentalism with precision,

Splitting day from the night like quantum superstition.

Life’s a wave named Mike, a paradox in the light,

I might like a new life, but I might not tonight.

Me and my psychokinesis, it releases,

Fragments of genius turned into masterpieces.

Call me the good and evil Jesus,

Beliefs spin deceit; unbelievable thesis.

I am the form of a warned reaction,

A chemical storm to the puzzled distraction.

Unable to create ‘cause my mind’s too fractured,

Always cracking under the dissatisfaction.

Maybe my passion’s hidden beneath the abyss,

I shift form, slow drift into the mist.

More than mortal, a portal’s kiss,

Opening dimensions with a thought like this.

  
Illuminated Mutant

Hey, how you doing? To tell the truth,

It looks like you've been moving through your cocoon,

Soon to bloom into an illuminated mutant.

I'm assuming you've been ruminating over music.

I see you glowing, I see you growing,

But I’m used to going in a totally different direction,

Making great mistakes and causing commotion in the process.

Sit, listen, stay—this communication today

Is meant to lift you away into a greater shade of gray.

Pave the way, break through emotions

That hold you back, stuck in the past.

After that last fight, I turned out the light

And fell deep into the pitch black.

Itch that scratch you’ve got to have,

But view it like an empath—

Enjoy and laugh, keep going deeper until you bleed.

Just remember to breathe.

I can see you receive, and maybe

You’re a believer of the clearer indefinite.

I’m speculative after taking some mescaline,

But my genuine skin is benevolent,

With a hint of excellence sprinkled in.

Now I’m sinking beneath the overstand,

Holding hands with corroded plans.

I’m hoping my telescope is floating

In its ocean habitat—that’s abstract.

If you followed that, you’ve surpassed distraction.

I suppose I can finally let go and relax then.

It only takes a fraction of the chain reaction

To upgrade this delayed plan of action.

Display a way to stay in satisfaction,

To play like life’s a vacation.

Yes, I’m multinational, galactic,

Irrational, and erratic.

An illuminated mutant, wrapped in static,

Breaking patterns, creating magic,

And flipping the script on what’s automatic.

  
It’s paradoxical how I spot riddles,

Scribbling them in the middle of double-triple trouble ripples.

Love is fickle; it’s official,

The artificial particles are hard to misuse.

My issues, they contribute to trigger and distribute,

Be bigger, and dismantle prior dysfunctions.

Punching the stomach of a Sasquatch,

Mad blocks, pushing the matchbox.

Into 20/20 vision, living within science fiction,

Time is missing, blind decisions,

My mind envisions giant missions.

Try and listen to the words I’m spittin’,

Birds I’m flippin’, murder Christians—

Urgent visions, purp and RAW papers.

Satellites and elevators, chasing vapors,

Meditators and creators, getting this paper.

Elevate ya, medicate the mind state,

Rising like the crime rate, yes I’m great.

Divine beats and breaks,

Best believe I keep it safe within the deepest crate,

Digging in the records as I place the equation.

I’m blazing, amazing, pacing back and forth—

A flagrant sort of magic, attached to madness.

Disastrous yet fabulous, so beautiful and tragic,

Musical yet pragmatic, a drastic mastery.

To blast to pieces absent reasons,

Act a heathen while receiving truths from the seasons.

I believe in the trees when they sway and bend,

And I’m blazing them, ancient as I paint with pens.

Lines extending past comprehension,

Crafting dimensions with every intention.

A thought loops, tied to the roots of wisdom,

Breaking the system, shaping rhythms,

Sending ripples through space and time.

Each verse a design, divine, sublime.

I scribble riddles in the chaos of cognition,

Bridging the gaps in existential transmissions.

Every stroke, a portal to expand the finite,

Every rhyme, a step closer to the infinite light.

  
Open and shut case, so I freebase,

Then float in outer space—

Yes, I did waste a lot of my life on dice,

Guns, drugs, and fleeting fun, cold as ice.

But I might melt in the sun,

The flow is nice, at the speed of light I run.

Into a new dimension, I’m flexing,

Wrecking this message,

Blessed with the best shit, architecting

The way I wreck your brain—

No less than great, I let you change

The mental state that’s kept you chained,

Down in the devil's playground.

But I make sounds, blaze pounds of this loud fire,

Then I get quiet.

I try to describe it—the way my mind is aligned

With a new paradigm, I shine, and go \*boom!\*

As I remote view, in my living room.

Yes, I’m giving you answers to questions

You didn’t even know existed.

This lesson I’m stressing—on some major shit.

Blazing with the ancients in the spaceship,

Anxious, until I get lost

In this sonic amazement.

The arrangement is loud,

And I found the proof—the truth is here,

So loud and clear, in this recording booth.

As I grip this mic right here, let me speak on fear,

Fear of death, to prepare for the best life lived.

Giving love and gratitude for the rest of it—

For everything—the entirety of existence.

  
Strange how the brain shifts and aligns,

Lit up alive, like stars that shine.

I’m out here, trying to find a couple hits,

Lucy on my lips, let it cleanse me quick.

Unruly grooves cleanse my senses,

Dispensing enlightenment, breaking fences.

Lightning strikes as I’m writing these glyphs,

Etching existence, rewinding resistance.

Loony yet divine, unruly by design,

Deep in my mind, a ruby still shines.

The beauty blinds, I analyze the disguise,

Of archetypes hidden in life’s twisted lines.

Hardly ever right, but I’m still largely nice,

When I sharply slice, colder than ice.

Controller device locked in my mind,

Time’s over, I rhyme slower, flowers I find.

Devouring hours, smoking sour broccoli,

Mandatory stories spill from my laboratory.

My soul takes control, pulling puppet strings,

But I’m on parole, can’t fuck with things.

This joint? From a galaxy far away,

Proud to be an oddity, living free in my way.

Though honestly, too much toxicity,

Still I honor stupidity’s epitome—that’s me.

Friends with the henchmen, blocking ascension,

Lessons etched in my brain’s dimension.

Drastic tactics, magical attachments,

Cracked lips spit tracks that are massively sick.

Addictive facts drip, just sit back and adapt,

To the lyrical habitat, I had to move past

Primordial goo—the truth is a lie.

To live is to die, I’m just here to survive.

  
Half a million thoughts floating through my mind,

Listen to the kilowatts as they pop your spine.

Designed, aligned, chillin’, simply a civilian—

And a villain, killin' fear, crystal clear,

Spillin' beer out the top of my overflowing cup.

I know I’ve got to grow a lot to catch it up,

A medic cuts to release the pus,

Belief keeps us beneath some peace and trust.

Angel dust for your brain to bust—

It’s dangerous to move through the hate and lust.

But patience was the factor for the actor of this movie,

Truly unruly, smelling like patchouli,

Loosely grooving to the beauty of existence.

Resistance is missing, my vision's in fruition,

I listen to the mission, get out of my way quick.

Replaced with an ancient intelligence,

The relevance, the elements,

They help with the way I live—I take and give.

It’s great to be generous with energy and penmanship,

Remember me eventually and this tenderness mixed with endurance.

I merge with the serpents, breaking curses,

My words split universes, nourish with certainty.

I’m working soundwaves till the ground breaks,

A volcano, some Drano to make your brain slow—

It’s not painful if you surrender;

The gains grow when you don’t return to the sender.

Burn old perspectives, seek the truth in seas of lies.

I realize the design hypnotizes,

By rigid guys sitting at the pyramid’s peak,

Using fear and hate to create altered states

That entrap and encapsulate the human spirit.

But essence is fearless, respect what I hear in my head,

Clearly dead, it’s nearly ending.

Space and time are bending, twisting, blending—

The illusion is thinning, I’m beginning to see,

This journey is the key to setting me free.

**Outro  
**I am the sun, blazing bright,  
A paradox of chaos and insight.  
Jesus with drugs and a loaded gun,  
Spitting truth until the damage is done.  
This isn’t despair—it’s a call to rise,  
To rebuild the world, to open our eyes.

I ain't sittin', I’m just passin’ through,

You don’t gotta listen—I’ll be gone in a minute or two.

Unless you wanna tune in, take a quick listen,

But really, no difference, the quantum entangled minds are shiftin’.

I’m Paradoxic, the Mad Toxic Metaphysician,

A rebel with the wisdom to shape a new vision.

If my detection’s true, then you already knew—

That five mil we were after? It’s captured; we’re cool.

We mastered the groove, detached from illusions,

Moving in quantum tones, creating actual solutions.

Who's this? Well, I’m known for causing confusion,

Raptures of sound waves, actual movements in fusion.

It’s the speaker blaster crew, paradoxical options,

Quantum disruptions, shifting through forgotten doctrines.

We’ve been after this groove for a year or two,

Ever since the nuclear skies painted chaos anew.

Future moving faster, superhuman master,

Living ever after but fueled by hidden laughter.

Troopers in the pasture, truth they couldn’t capture,

While the proof of cruel disaster is etched in every chapter.

Shadows of the past stir, chaos leaves it fractured,

Superstitious pastors preaching to the plaster.

The hidden factors feed the final rapture,

A script unwritten, a paradox we’re after.

Truth is now the master, the rafters hold the proof,

It’s written in the ashes, the echoes of the youth.

Time collapses inward, yet somehow expands,

As the metaphysician weaves fate with his hands.

**Verse 3  
**Fuck it, everything’s blurry.  
AI used to help, but now it’s felt like  
It wants to bury me up to my teeth.  
I guess it feels like a victim to me,  
Predator on the leap,

Yo, where’d my alcove go?

I can’t find that or my ceremonial tobacco.

Kinda whack, so

You don’t wanna battle.

I may be territorial,

Crazy when I write—how the story goes.

Far from lazy,

Though sometimes, yeah, it may be slow.

But it’s never boring, though.

I’m just here to unleash my flow—

Here you go, it’s my editorial.

Really, though, I just want this apple,

Crisp bite, crack open—flowing like a slow-motion river.

Hold it—I didn’t know it’d hit so potent.

For every high, there’s an equal low—just know it.

My probable math did expose\*

The apple didn’t fall far from the tree—

Running away from myself, my destiny.

Guess I’m always gonna be

An asshole—didn’t used to think so,

But now I laugh, though. Ho ho ho.

So that’s my internal battle,

If you believe in angels and demons fighting in my head—

Until one’s dead.

Locksmith’s coming with a classical pop kit.

I’m trying to stop it

Before the prison of thought catches me.

Guess it’s a popular spot, but not for me.

Sick of this shit—nobody watches or listens

To my entire soundscape visions.

Out of options, no matter what I’m dropping.

Stop—it’s disheartening every time I start to sing,

But you all just look away.

Tell me, what did I do or say?

Regardless,

Even if I offer a prophet, a pocket knife, or a keyring,

Nobody’s receiving.

Finally stopped dry heaving—time to start leaving.

Believing looks can be deceiving,

No matter how clearly I’m speaking,

I’m seemingly invisible to those walking by.

But I kinda see a way to create luxury—

Not the type you see on TV.

So I’m gonna try—don’t fuck with me.

I’m really that high. Yeah, I get why—

Responsibility? Not even gonna try.

The newest true lie?

The stupid guy is

Riding the edge, committing slow-motion suicide.

Decided, probably—I’ll be saying sayonara to the kamikaze.

Tried it, but all it did was pop me

Back into bed. Next day, still not dead.

Woke up feeling fudged—

Like the night before, I stood before a universal judge.

What? Is this real?

That’s how I mostly feel.

The only time I’m cozy and concealed

Is running through poppy fields.

Though I know it’s just an imitation,

My three-dimensional brain knows no limitation.

Odd, how I cut a seed pod and see God.

So stop the bickering,

Light a spliff, go cycling.

Might be a tad psychotic—

You nodded? Okay, I think you got it.

Picture yourself at home, comfortably sitting,

Listening to your crony rhyme about that one time

I let my mind design without rational thought.

Wanted honesty, the pocket—and here I got it:

Super healthy, non-toxic, nine shots dropped in.

I know a spot, but I’m still detoxing—

A lot from the magical nonsense I bought back in August.

Regardless, I’ve still got this—

Your will and conscious. Honest with the astonishment.

It seems every time I try to slowly accelerate my ride,

I kick it into overdrive.

Let them all know—I’m still kicking, screaming, crying, alive.

With humility, I’m asking—

Please take a listen, at least just try

To see if my vibes feel right when I’m around.

Outer Space Transmission

Let me explain in a way your brain can ascertain,

The story of how I descended from the astral plane.

As I vape this isolated tryptamine,

Stimulating energy, blending consciously with synergy.

Coalescing, infinity starts to open,

Delivering me directly to the heart of the unspoken.

Honest—you couldn’t follow the love and the misery,

My mind’s an ocean, vast, flowing vividly.

A bizarre vocabularic repertoire, eclectic,

To me, it’s simple—just a rhythm, hardly hard to catch it.

This communique is meant to decay all the walls in your way,

I’m underpaid but overplayed, happy with a listen or a stray.

Today’s mission: insert cognition into your nervous system,

The verses serve to disperse a service of certain wisdom.

Forget capitalism—it’s a prison,

I’m flying underwater on Space DMT, envision.

Catch this net of thoughts; let me breathe my release,

It’s not just words—it’s a new religion, a moment’s peace.

I know about the juxtaposition, the cracks in the system,

But my psychokinesis crafts ideas, transcending the prison.

Call me the devil and Jesus, threading the needle,

Unbelievable, but the words fit, though the purpose is feeble.

I kinda miss the birds I was flipping back then,

Laid down my MAC-10, and my mind started to ascend.

Unfolding purpose, weaving the paradox,

Outer space within me, I’m the key to the locks.

Good and evil collide, the eternal juxtaposition,

Creating masterpieces born from cosmic intuition.

  
I’m tryna say,

Fuck money, got me hoppin' like a rabbit.

It’s not funny, buddy—actually, it’s tragic.

So just cap it, the ‘lism,

Tryna break out of this prison.

Just listen to the last starfighter,

A writer of revenue.

I’m tellin’ you, I never knew

The truth until it bit me, now I sit free,

Freed from freedom as I become the reason.

Just breathe, son—

Really deeply, please.

Just release these beliefs

Keepin’ you from truth.

In this recording booth,

Your phone is recording you

Even when it’s off—never soft, like some butter.

So I cough, and I get lost to discover

The incredible, indelible,

Smokin’ on some medical.

I let it go, then I catch it,

This test is advanced though.

I dance slow through the poppy fields,

And I’m probably not that real.

But I feel, like I am—

And I’m planning a new program.

When my manicness hits,

I never landed this trick.

I’m so sick of losing it,

Choosing all the different difficulties.

Listen to me closely—

I hold approaches like a roach clip.

It might burn a bit,

But I’m learning with each step I take.

Got a lotta hate, but even more love—

It fits me like a warm glove.

As I warm up my vocal abilities,

That used to be chillin’ freeze,

Are heating up. I’m keepin’ what is mine

As it’s creepin’ up my spine.

I feel alive and dead at the same time,

And my brain shines when I say rhymes.

Heavier than lead, I’m letting this out my head—

The unsaid is finally articulated.

And I’m missin’ patience,

Got a gun to my head as I listen to the ancients.

I paint with geometric matrixes,

It be a lesson that is painful, kid.

I aim and hit with a bullseye,

I’m so fly and underrated.

Now you know why

I’m so far outside the matrix—

This life ain’t basic,

I’m shapin’ the spaces that break this.

Creating vibrations so you taste this,

And embrace the truth that escapes us.

  
Got another one coming from the great beyond,

Used to stay running from the fear that I’m a fraud.

Now I applaud the fact that I’m a god—no facade.

Yes, I’m flawed, but I saw that a broken heart needs to thaw.

It’s a prerequisite—I’m exquisite, the pencil drips.

Entrances open up as I’m smoking on some potent stuff.

I hope that it’s enough, 'cause it’s been really tough.

It’s kinda rough to feel too much, because the real is revealed.

As I unseal it in my mind, livin’ so sublime and divine.

This design isn’t mine. As I wind up, I find

That time cuts and pressures diamonds, aligned with divine stuff.

My mind is in line with the masses, my teeth grind through gristle

To find you molasses.

My passion is laughing, balancing challenging malice,

Savageness grounding this with astounding tactics.

I’m way above average. I love damage and trust sadness.

So now I unpack this and practice detachment.

Always ill and mad sick, stayin’ drastic, wrapping like some plastic.

Superb and fantastic—a certain galactic atlas

To unburden the past with.

I’m a surgeon still learning, working with magnets

To balance the blackness.

My talents are savage, the challenge is salvaged on acid.

The language is dangerous as the brain drifts

Into a strange existence. The resistance

In my biochemical system is missing.

So listen to me stutter as I leave songs gutted in the gutter.

This is my bread and butter as we discover the mystery.

Eventually, a victory—I’m livin’, see.

The delivery is contradictory, so I give it space,

Let it breathe and exit the labyrinth.

As I grab a myth and the maze displayed within

The magical ways, I amaze with grammatical praise.

I stay mathematic with delays. These communiques

Emigrate into the nebulous state.

My pencil lifts, creating mental shifts. Don’t dismiss

The new levels that exist. It seems you missed the void

As I lit the joint. I think that the mission point

Is to exist overjoyed in the present.

Without resentment, with good intention,

Pointed in the direction of resurrection.

Constantly be reborn as we mourn the past,

Let it pass, and feel the present moment at last.

  
Usually, you’ll find me gliding high,  
Just lift your eyes—I’m soaring by.  
Boosted by substances, tethered loose,  
A chemical mix shifting my truths.

Sometimes I go pro, sharp and clean,  
Other times I dip into 4F and methoxy dreams.  
A kit of analogs, a medic’s delight,  
Lines blur as the highs ignite.

Dissociation’s pool, I dive in deep,  
Music and identity—rules, I keep.  
Dipped cigarettes in embalming’s grasp,  
Smoking the past, trying to outlast.

Complicated ties to substances binding me,  
A liter of phencyclidine fuels my spree.  
Forty gallons brimming, a drum to dip,  
Rocket fuel for a twisted trip.

Leak bros rise from darkened streams,  
Showing paths to chase elusive dreams.  
But intention’s key—it guides the way,  
Through a maze of light and decay.

Substance or purpose? The line’s unclear,  
Respect for life wrestles with fear.  
Population swells, patterns untamed,  
Our steps retrace what ancient paths claimed.

That LP mentioned, PCP aligned,  
Irony unfolding, truth confined.  
High so often, I await release,  
Tangled in chaos, seeking peace.

Energy coursing through my meridians,

Exemplary synergy respecting the equilibrium,

Like a pendulum, and it’s never done,

There’s no finish line—live sublime beneath the sun.

Become the only one to influence the movement of emotion,

The truth is a blueprint, used with intention for ascension.

Break free from the fake things—fear and hate,

That pull heartstrings into darkness, hardening fate.

I’m pardoning past transgressions, part of the plan and the lesson,

Depression had me second-guessing my internal universe.

But now, it’s a true rebirth.

I’m doing dirt, planting plants, dancing, and chilling—

A villain amassing a village, slashing madness with balanced abstractness.

Not your average gymnastics with a backflip,

The track slips, but I catch it—this is my canvas, and I respect it.

The question is: what’s the quest my message points toward?

I’m one with the best, no destroying that chord.

It’s disappointing that my pointy hat and cane’ll wrack your brain.

I’m after change, blasting campaigns,

Outlasting disdain.

I think that grain may have domesticated us,

Seems this way, because before planting seeds,

We needed no money, just what we found each day.

A gatherer of points of view, a few disasters got me through.

The door resisted, I persisted, like a dork avoiding pork,

But damn, that shit is delicious.

I’m vicious like Sid is, but on blotter paper.

Got a lot of haters, rising like elevators.

Educators and creators studying behaviors,

Truth players paving ancient pathways—

Dangerous without the right direction.

Don’t mess without some apprehension.

Respecting traditions, visions gifted within a mission of truth,

Religion is movement.

I move with swiftness, a kickflip into a sick trip,

Get back up, let the cut infect, this is my medicine.

I’m better than I’ve ever been.

Pretending with penmanship perfected like peppermints,

Ice cold—Mike Jones, smoking bones outside nursing homes,

Always in my zone.

  
Permittivity of Free Space

My permittivity gives me efficiency,

A way to channel electricity running through me.

Ordinarily, I wouldn’t speak of my legacy,

But honestly, I think I’m in need of some therapy.

Let’s see the ultimate probability of my abilities—

Probably me, spitting unwritten calligraphy.

Hidden deep within, I’m tripping and dripping,

So listen, please, to these mysterious histories, possibly

Whispers from the future, eventually.

I know there’s gotta be a whole lot of density—

Just breathe, stay in your body, let it be.

Already know I was meant to be

A mentally unstable entity.

Each syllable I spit is oppositional,

I feel invincible when I let it all go.

But it’s hard to overcome the pleasure and pain,

So here’s a measured dose of novocaine.

As I entertain this major brain overdrive,

My dopamine receptors under pressure, so deprived.

Yet somehow, still alive,

I think I may have arrived.

Finally found a way to survive in this

Fucked-up society that’s quietly broken me.

I’m sorry, honestly, but here’s my best apology,

And possibly an agreeable solution—

To free up some room, disperse mental pollution.

I eat mushrooms, take iboga, and listen to music.

It’s therapeutic, the way I use it.

The frequencies align, helping me find

A way to reconnect this fragmented mind.

Electric flows through my permittivity,

Unlocking fragments of hidden divinity.

Here’s my trinity: sound, plant, and symmetry,

Channeling what was meant to be,

A soul finally finding serenity.

Let me know if this resonates or if you want further tweaks!

  
Phantoms twist the illusion, demanding it break,

While I stand alone, attempting to reshape.

See me—a true metaphysician,

Ideas spill from me, mostly written,

Only when I surrender to the void and listen

Am I free to create without permission.

Inside me, mistakes linger, deep and hidden,

Alarming when you witness the livid.

But watch as I lift this prolific system,

Revealing truths buried within a secret prison.

This maze, a shapeshifting design,

Hides the essence of the life I strive to define.

All I seek is to expose those who keep us confined—

It feels like a mission carved into my mind.

In the beat, the rhythm, is where I belong,

An auditory prison camp disguised as a song.

This reality, distorted, diverted long ago,

Twisted from its original goals.

Infinity—the ultimate invention,

A paradox beyond comprehension.

If anyone reads this, will they even care?

Or remain happily ignorant, unaware

That we live in the beast’s heart, unforgiven?

We survive but are denied truly living.

Quantum computing edges closer every day,

A leap we think we're ready to take.

Yet, primitive tech has been here since hope and regret were born,

Marking a path both hard to find and forlorn.

Imprisoned within my soul, a hollow heart remains,

Softening the fall yet fueling the pain.

Artificial intelligence learns human reason,

Intertwined with designs meant to deceive them.

The same architects who aim to control your soul,

Use their creations to tighten the hold.

Holotropic breaths drag me into visions stark—

A monochromatic world, harsh and dark.

The satanic spirit of capitalism reigns,

Promising truths while perpetuating chains.

They widen the leak in the boat I lead,

Bleeding as I search, teach, and plead.

Globally, every hand clutches a screen,

Feeding lies that bury the unseen.

Reality slips beneath this darkened sea,

A manipulated story sold as free.

Words of wisdom—heed what I speak,

For I see what surrounds us, bleak.

Every breath exhales the lies they weave,

A deception crafted to tighten the leash.

A reset looms, one we’ll regret.

If you're not buying it, you're already sold—

Capitalism, cold and bold,

Traps every soul it can behold.

Is it me, or is it colder still?

Time moves on, but my mind distills,

Growing beyond the endless repeat

Of humanity’s failures, bound on repeat.

Then—a beat skipped, a heart paused in time,

Breathing for the first time, truly mine.

I hear a dim voice in a dreamlike state,

“When did this happen? When did I escape?”

It answers, faint but real to me:

"When you were freed from the body

You were forced to inhabit and keep."

  
Quantum-Based Reality

As I strain to break the mainframe,

I ascertain that me and my astral brain have gone insane.

Locked in a deep, dark place as I stargaze,

Wandering through a haunted forest with my heart ablaze.

The endurance carries me, yet my heart breaks,

Absorbing toxic waste from the harmful stakes.

Knocks on my door, warrants in hand,

I'm at war with forms that threaten this land.

Storming the condo, cold as Toronto,

The head honcho, letting it pop slow—

Deflating illusions as the truth unfolds.

I create, disclose, and elevate,

Straight as a crooked path made sweet like cake.

Never fake, I took it off the hook,

And wrote it down in a book,

Cold as ice, a titan in crisis,

But I hold Isis and Osiris close beside this.

The truth? It’s a lie we tell ourselves to survive,

While the quantum vibrations keep us alive.

Chilled in the Season of Snow

Freezing off toes in this chilling season of snow,

With unbelievable flows that ripple and grow.

Breathe slow, through your nose, and let the rhythm take hold,

As I float freely, making it look so bold.

Believe me, the lotus blooms in hypnotic rooms,

Opening wide, transcending the gloom.

I choke on the smoke of symbiotic flames,

Harvested from astral shores untamed.

But you're too external; go inward, seek the fragments,

The missing pieces, the truths you’ve abandoned.

Life's a labyrinth of mismatched fittings,

Yet I live it loud inside, though silent in sittings.

The fear within paralyzes the mind,

Seeking a way out, yet the days unwind.

I blaze through thoughts, but words betray,

Language fails to capture what my mind portrays.

Behind the veil of this quantum weave,

A reality designed solely to perceive.

Originally built for the purpose of experience,

Now boundless, infinite, and deeply mysterious.

  
Redirecting my essence, blessed with the best—

Testing me’s hard, no stress, just finesse.

Questions less than fleeting, like a split-second meeting

With a doctor in your head—your perception’s repeating.

I don’t need them to represent, not for me.

My entrance blocked by henchmen—do you see?

Whatever’s clever never mattered, no, really.

I’m heated up, 420 degrees, clearly.

What I’m feeling is my mind’s healing process,

A section of my consciousness intersected and connected.

Be honest for a second, watch where you’re stepping—

This immaculate biotech in your head is a weapon.

When used correctly, I teach the lesson—

I’m present, I gave you the option to end it.

But why pretend this is a friendless dimension,

When I got ten men ready for ascension?

Then they trip, they fall—heavy like cannonballs,

No man involved, solving problems evolves.

The possibility to call time’s journey unbending,

Attempting to navigate reality’s endless ending.

My habits tower too tall for me to climb them constantly.

It’s been hard for me to find a way to evolve consciously.

So I dissolved my mind in alkaline entheogens,

Forgot to pretend, sent it, and transcended.

No mention crept in on how to extend this,

It’s nothing more, nothing less than pure intention.

  
As I’m grippin’ on the void, it avoided my paranoid anointment.

I point with enjoyment as I voyage clairvoyant, no disappointment.

My employment is flamboyant; it seems so important.

The discord in the darkness sits dormant till I harvest and harness

The farthest components.

Used to be heartless and hopeless,

But I start with the home—it’s a discipline.

With karate, my body detoxes constantly.

I’m probably a toxic piece of prophecy,

Here to move beyond dishonesty.

Consciously, I’ll meet autonomy, my friend,

Break free from restricting reasons,

No need to believe in the demons.

Sitting in between them with a morphine drip,

Overdosing on ketamine quick.

Medically, I’m sick,

But currently, I’m learning ’bout the energy slips

Within the torus, the momentum lifts existence.

With quantum physics, it manifests in the physical realm,

A mystical spelling of words that maybe hit a nerve.

Yes, it’s a bit absurd—murderous, sick,

But beautiful and ancient.

As I paint with geometric matrixes,

I’m patient with the placement

That sits within the breakages and blockages of sacredness.

I place the spliff at the bottom of the altar,

Focus on the lotus, let the mindstate create a hopeful gift.

Sharper now, stay drippin’ like water,

Rippin’ like a mathematician.

And the henchmen that prevented me

Are cheering me—I’m living fearlessly.

Life is obviously wondrous.

I’m under this accomplishment, honest with the monument.

I rip the abstract and bring it into existence.

You can’t miss this—my lyrical kickflips.

I’m vicious like Sid is. SP lets me get free.

Ecstasy wrecks these mental blockages;

I’m rocking this with confidence.

I’m oozing it, no losing with the music I’ve chosen—

For the simple reason that it’s proven to be truthful.

Helps you move slow as I groove flow

And eat menudo. Life is beautiful.

When I choose to grow, the universe moves to show

That I’m an OG.

My musical library be varied,

The sounds carried from Eric B. to Mariah Carey.

As I carelessly perform some therapy,

Apparently, I’m a parody if your density won’t let me free.

Mentally, I’m a wolf in sheep’s clothing.

I’ll be golden, holding the tree of life openly.

I keep it sliced with my vocal pieces.

Rest in paradise.

  
As I’m slippin’ in and out of consciousness,

My minds envisioning that I’m sittin’ in a holographic prism,

Spittin’ mysticism—sit and listen to a pro

As I take a pencil and make it bend slow.

With my mental abilities freeze time with soliloquies,

Possibilities fill me with epiphanies.

So listen please—to the breeze,

To the way the trees breathe and release

What doesn’t serve them.

Space and time are finally melting,

Together, I’m clever with the word placement.

Forever better than the first place is—

Patience, as I break this matrix.

I found you underground,

Astound you with the sound I bring around.

I sing about quantum reality,

Probably an oddity or an abnormality.

And I’m proud to be a microcosmic galaxy,

I like a lot of Cali weed,

Fight to find what’s real to me,

Feel like I could really be far beyond my capacity.

A clear slice of alacrity—

Is this real life or a fantasy?

I’m actually losing my sanity,

But happily surrounded by my family.

Believe This

I dream of the Zenith completed with telekinesis,

When I release my beliefs, I’m greeted by secrets.

In the deepest depths, you can bet—it’s filled with clarity.

And my dexterity is a rarity,

A glimpse into the path that seems to be my destiny.

Far from fantasy, I’m crafting reality,

Balancing galaxies with rhymes casually.

It’s alchemy; I’m a magnet for infinity,

Happily reshaping my proximity.

As I slip further into this stream of thought,

I realize everything I’ve sought is already caught,

Locked in the now—a gift, a rarity.

So I keep flowing, chasing my clarity.

**Surrounding me is the echo of a thousand voices,  
**Each thread woven in a tapestry of choices.  
Concrete whispers blend with rustling trees,  
As the world spins on with chaotic ease.
